# Player cards - every rookie, 2000-2022

What the system would have said after each player's first 25 games, using only players it had never seen (out-of-fold for classes up to ~2013, walk-forward by class after that), and what really happened. VORP is summed over years 3 and 4. Style comparables come from all eras.


## 2000 draft class

#### Kenyon Martin - #1 pick, 2000
- First games: 33.6 mpg in 25 of 25 team games, age 23
- Pick alone expects **5.1** VORP (years 3+4) | model **3.8** | floor -0.0, median 2.8, ceiling 7.0
- Tiers: Replacement level 30% · Bench 12% · Rotation 22% · Starter 6% · All-Star level 30% (~50 similar players)
- Looks like: Evan Mobley (#3, 2021), Larry Sanders (#15, 2010), Taj Gibson (#26, 2009)
- ⚠️ Warning - heavy minutes, weak team: historically the model was too optimistic for this profile by 0.7 VORP on average (90% range -1.7 to +0.3, 27 players)
- **What happened:** 4.2 VORP -> Starter
